SELECT u.UserId, 'SuperAdmin', NOW()

INSERT INTO user_roles (user_id, role_name, created_at)
SELECT u.UserId, 'SuperAdmin', NOW()
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN user_roles ur ON ur.user_id = u.UserId
WHERE ur.id IS NULL
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_roles WHERE role_name = 'SuperAdmin')
ORDER BY u.UserId
LIMIT 1;
